Let's derive using intervals: high I=[0,θ], low J=[θ,1]. Convolution of step with itself: it's the sum of convolutions of indicators: H^2 (I*I)+HL(I*J+J*I)+L^2(J*J). Since all on same [0,1] linear convolution. The indicator convolutions are triangular/trapezoidal.
I*I: support [0,2θ], triangular: for s≤θ: s; for θ<s≤2θ:2θ-s.
J*J: J=[θ,1], shift by θ: support [2θ,2], triangular with length n=1-θ: for s∈[2θ,1+θ]: s-2θ; for s∈[1+θ,2]:2-s. (if θ<1/2 maybe order; in general length n).
Cross I*J: support [θ,1+θ], trapezoid? I length θ, J length 1-θ separated; I*J is convolution of [0,θ] and [θ,1] shifted. Let J shifted left by θ: [0,1-θ]. So I*J = convolution of intervals [0,θ] and [0,1-θ], support [0,1], shifted by θ. So shape: if θ≤1-θ (θ≤0.5): triangular up to θ, plateau θ from θ to 1-θ, down to 0 at 1; shifted: increases [θ,2θ], plateau 2θ to 2-θ, decreases 2-θ to 1+θ. If θ≥0.5 similar plateau length 2θ-1? Cross term doubled J*I same.

```
import numpy as np, time

def _two_block_score(n,m): # H=2,L=1
    q=n-m
    if q<=0 or m<=0: return np.inf
    # compute max b over s 0..2n-2
    maxb=0.0
    for s in range(2*n-1):
        T = s+1 if s <= n-1 else 2*n-1-s
        # a high-high triangular support 0..2m-2
        if s < m: a=s+1
        elif s <= 2*m-2: a=2*m-1-s
        else: a=0
        # c low-low: r=s-2m, support r 0..2q-2
        r=s-2*m
        if 0 <= r < q: c=r+1
        elif q <= r <= 2*q-2: c=2*q-1-r
        else: c=0
        b = 2*T + 2*a - c  # for H=2,L=1? Let's verify: b=4a + c + 2*(T-a-c)=2T+2a-c. yes.
        if b>maxb: maxb=b
    S=2*m+q
    return 2.0*n*maxb/(S*S)
```

But we can choose nonuniform weights! In a set of size m, diagonal sums are weighted pair products. Even if many pair sums collide, we can assign weights to reduce max. Let's revisit LP strategy but with better objectives. The state of art approach: fix f, solve LP maximizing sum(g) subject to f*g ≤ M. This is like finding the largest sequence under current convolution bound. Starting from all ones, degenerate. But one can break degeneracy by using a nonuniform start (e.g., exponential) and regularizing. Then iterate. Could converge to a local optimum with score maybe target. Let's implement this LP with the dual O(n) structure and a way to recover g. Maybe easier than projected gradient.

Given f normalized sum S (we can keep sum constant), M=max(f*f). LP: maximize sum(g) subject to f*g ≤ M, g≥0. The optimal value M*sum(y) by dual. The primal solution g can be any maximizer. If we choose an extreme point, it may be sparse and bad. But we can pick a particular optimal g: the one closest to f, or with a small second moment, by solving a secondary LP/QP.

Since we only need a direction, we can solve the LP to get dual y, then use complementary slackness to find a g in the nullspace. The set of optimal g satisfies: for j where (A^T y)_j >1, g_j=0; for k where y_k>0, (f*g)_k=M. We can choose g to minimize ||g-f||^2 subject to these equalities/inequalities. This is a QP with possibly many variables. But maybe we can instead solve LP with objective max sum(g) - ε ||g||_2^2? Not LP. Or add a linear term: maximize sum(g) + δ ∑ h_j g_j with h chosen to bias. We can set h_j = f_j (or entropy) to get a smoother unique solution. Solving LP with objective 1^T g + δ f^T g? Still linear, may choose supports. Add small random perturbations to break ties in favor of smoothing? For f all ones, any feasible g with sum≤n; objective sum(g) + δ∑ w_j g_j will put mass where w high, causing spikes, not smooth. We want distribute.

Alternatively, solve a convex problem directly: minimize M? Maybe use the dual to define a gradient for a barrier.

Another idea: The primal LP solutions include the uniform g if f uniform, which is a maximizer (sum=n) and smooth. Pseudo:
```
def make_func(y, beta):
    y = y - (y > 700).??
    if not finite: return inf, None
    y = y - np.median(y) # stable softmax
    p = np.exp(y); p /= p.sum()
    Q = np.convolve(p,p)
    # stable logsumexp
    bq = beta*Q - beta*Q.max()
    z = np.sum(np.exp(bq))
    val = 2*m*( (Q.max() + np.log(z/K))/beta )
    bq2 = beta*(Q-Q.max())
    w = np.exp(bq2); w/=w.sum()
    W = np.convolve(w, np.ones(m), mode='valid')  # length m? w len 2m-1, ones len m => m
    h = np.correlate(w, p, mode='full')[:m]  # check orientation
    gp = (W*p + h)/beta
    gy = p*(gp - np.dot(p,gp))
    return val, gy
```

def fft_max_conv(x):
   L=1
   while L < 2*len(x)-1: L*=2
   fx=np.fft.rfft(x,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fx*fx, L)[:2n-1]; return max(c)
```

```
def local_search(p, budget, rng, seed_best):
   n=len(p); best=p; best_score=score_fft(p)
   widths = [4,8,16,32,64,128]
   sigma=0.3
   iter=0
   while time < deadline:
      choose width, start, new=best.copy(); new[start:start+w] *= exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
      new/=sum(new)
      s=score_fft(new)
      if s<best_score: best=new; best_score=s; no_improve=0; sigma*=0.999
      else: no_improve++; if no_improve large: reduce sigma; occasionally restart? Also try block affine: scale block so sum? 
   return best,best_score
```

Detailed code plan:

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _project(v, target=1.0): ...
def _coarse_score(p):
    # p length m sum maybe not 1; normalize? We'll keep sum1.
    q = np.convolve(p,p)
    return 2.0*len(p)*q.max()
def _coarse_fgrad(y, m, beta):
    y = np.asarray(y)
    if not finite: return (inf,None)
    y = y - np.median(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -50, 50)
    p = np.exp(y); p /= p.sum()
    q = np.convolve(p,p)
    K = len(q)
    bq = beta*(q - q.max())
    e = np.exp(bq)
    z = e.sum()
    val = 2.0*m*(q.max() + np.log(z/K)/beta)
    w = e/z
    W = np.convolve(w, np.ones(m), mode='valid')
    h = np.correlate(w, p, mode='full')[:m]
    gp = (W*p + h)/beta
    gy = p*(gp - float(np.dot(p,gp)))
    return val, gy
```
Need val uses 2*m*LME, where LME = qmax + log(z/K)/beta. Because sum exp(β(q-qmax))/K = exp(βqmax)*.../K; log/ZK. So log(mean exp(βq)) = β qmax + log(e.sum()/K)? Wait e = exp(β(q-qmax)); mean exp(βq)=exp(βqmax)*e.mean(). log = βqmax + log(e.sum()/K). Divide beta: qmax + log(e.sum()/K)/beta. Yes.

But if beta small, logsumexp good. If K=127. Good.

`np.convolve(w, ones, 'valid')` returns length (2m-1)-m+1=m. Good.

Full score via FFT:
```
def full_score(p):
   # p sum maybe1, nonnegative
   s=p.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return inf
   p = p/s
   L = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
   fx = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
   c = np.fft.irfft(fx*fx, L)[:2*n-1]
   return 2*n*c.max()
```

def _random_profile(rng,m):
   kind=rng.integers(4)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   if kind==0:
      mu=rng.uniform(0,1); sig=rng.uniform(0.05,0.4); v=np.exp(-((t-mu)/sig)**2)
   elif kind==1:
      mu1,rng... two gaussians
   elif kind==2:
      a=rng.uniform(-3,3); b=rng.uniform(-2,2); c=rng.uniform(-2,2); d=rng.uniform(-3,3); v=(1+a*t+b*t*t+c*t**3)*np.exp(d*t)
   else:
      v=rng.uniform(0.2,1,m); smooth: v=np.convolve(v, np.ones(5)/5, mode='same')
   v=np.clip(v,1e-6,None)
   y=np.log(v)-np.mean(np.log(v))
   return np.clip(y,-6,6)
```

def _full_local(x, deadline, rng):
    n=len(x)
    p=x/x.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_s=full_score(p)
    # exact evaluator uses clipping; p max1.
    widths = [4,8,16,32,64,128,256]
    sigma = 0.25
    no_imp=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # choose smooth block perturbation
       w = widths[rng.integers(len(widths))]
       st = rng.integers(0, n-w)
       # generate log multiplier: maybe ramp to zero at edges
       mult = rng.normal(0, sigma, w)
       # smooth mult by cumsum? Apply taper
       taper = np.linspace(0,1,w); taper = np.minimum(taper, 1-taper+?) ; if w>1: taper = np.where(taper<0.2, taper/0.2, np.minimum(1,taper/(0.2)))? Simpler: mult *= np.hanning(w)
       logp = np.log(best+1e-300)
       logp[st:st+w] += mult
       new = np.exp(logp - logp.max()); new/=new.sum()
       s=full_score(new)
       if finite and s<best_s*(1-1e-12): accept; no_imp=0; sigma=max(0.01,sigma*0.98)
       else:
          no_imp+=1; if no_imp>200: sigma*=0.7; no_imp=0
          # occasionally apply global smooth perturbation: frequency random
    return best*1000? We'll scale to max1 for return.
```

Implementation details for full score:
```
def _full_score(p):
   n=p.size
   s=p.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return inf
   p=p/s
   L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
   fx=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fx*fx, L)[:2*n-1]
   # FFT small negative? clip max
   return float(2*n*max(c.max(),0.0))
```
This score assumes sum1. If p sum not1, it normalizes each eval; local p maintained sum1.

Potential performance: Computing L from bit_length each call. Precompute L outside.

def score_full(p, L):
   fx=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=... 
```
Use `fx * fx` (not abs square; for real convolution, product of DFT with itself, not conjugate. Yes polynomial square. `irfft(fx*fx)` gives linear convolution? For autocorrelation we'd use conj, but convolution uses phase square. So `fx*fx` not `fx*np.conjugate(fx)`. Good.

Maybe a 3-level profile with heights optimized can reach 1.5. We can include random step profiles as starts. Generate p with 3 blocks random heights (log) and fractions; add to starts. L-BFGS will smooth them. If a step profile itself has good score, start true score considered.

Let's add start generation for random multi-block (3 or 4) with random log heights and block lengths. For each, p is piecewise constant, sum normalized, evaluate true score; also as y. This may find coarse <1.5 directly.

In code, `random_multiblock` maybe 40 starts. Too many; okay.

Could there be a simple 3-block continuous optimum? Let's attempt by reasoning but not needed.

We should limit code length. The final code will be maybe 250 lines. Accept.

Let's verify gradient formula one more time. Objective LME = (1/β) log( (1/K) ∑ exp(β Q_k) ). p sum1. Q_k=∑_i p_i p_{k-i}. Derivative of Q_k wrt p_i = p_{k-i}+p_i for valid. If k-i=i, p_i+p_i=2p_i, correct. If k-i invalid, both invalid, 0. Good.
Then derivative wrt p (unconstrained) is gp=(1/β)∑ w_k ∂Q_k/∂p_i. We compute W_i*p_i (from p_i term) + h_i (from p_{k-i} term). The derivative of LME wrt p not account for constraint sum p=1. We transform to y: p softmax. For any differentiable f(p) along p(y)=exp(y)/Z, gradient w.r.t y: p_i (∂f/∂p_i - ∑ p_j ∂f/∂p_j). We do that. Good. For softmax, adding constant to y no change; gradient sum zero. Good.

L-BFGS with y unbounded but gradient has zero sum; okay.

`val` constant offset `-log K/β` not affect gradient. Good.

Potential issue: For large β, w concentrated on max Q, LME approximates maxQ but second-order terms? fine.

Now, local full score uses FFT. The convolution of p with itself may have tiny negative due to FFT; max positive. Use `np.maximum(c,0).max()`.

In full local, if p has extremely small sum due underflow, redivide. We can clip p positive.

Let's write final code. Ensure no text outside fence. Use only allowed imports. `import numpy as np, time, scipy.optimize`.

One important rule: "It is called with NO arguments, so your default for budget_s is the one that runs." Our function signature default 1500.

Let's produce code. Need be careful with variable names and dense. We'll write in final.
</think>

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _project_to_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    n = len(v)
    x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1e12, neginf=-1e12)
    x.sort()
    ss = np.cumsum(x)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1)
    cond = x + ss / rho > target / n
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    rho_ind = int(np.searchsorted(cond, False))
    theta = (ss[rho_ind] - target) / float(rho_ind)
    out = np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)
    out /= out.sum()
    return out * target


def _softmax(y):
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    y = y - float(np.median(y))
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
    p = np.exp(y)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full_like(p, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def _coarse_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    return float(2.0 * p.size * max(float(q.max()), 0.0))


def _coarse_fgrad(y, m, beta):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size != m or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return (np.inf, None)
    y = y - float(np.median(y))
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
    p = np.exp(y)
    ps = p.sum()
    if ps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
        return (np.inf, None)
    p = p / ps

    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    K = q.size
    qmax = float(q.max())
    bz = beta * (q - qmax)
    ez = np.exp(bz)
    z = ez.sum()
    if z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(z):
        return (np.inf, None)
    val = float(2.0 * m * (qmax + float(np.log(z / float(K))) / beta))
    w = ez / z

    W = np.convolve(w, np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64), mode="valid")
    h = np.correlate(w, p, mode="full")[:m]
    gp = (W * p + h) / beta
    gy = p * (gp - float(np.dot(p, gp)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
        return (np.inf, None)
    return val, gy


def _opt_coarse_start(y0, m, beta, deadline):
    y0 = np.asarray(y0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    y0 = y0 - float(np.median(y0))
    y0 = np.clip(y0, -8.0, 8.0)

    def fg(y):
        return _coarse_fgrad(y, m, beta)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fg,
            y0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": 120,
                "maxfun": 250,
                "ftol": 1e-9,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            res = None
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return y0


def _coarse_polish(p, deadline, rng):
    m = p.size
    best = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_s = _coarse_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best = np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
        best_s = _coarse_score(best)

    for _ in range(8000):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        y = np.log(best + 1e-300)
        w = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, m // 2)))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, m - w + 1)))
        sig = float(rng.choice((0.15, 0.35, 0.8)))
        y[st:st + w] += rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=w)
        cand = _softmax(y)
        s = _coarse_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best = cand
            best_s = s
    return best, best_s


def _make_random_profile(rng, m):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
    if kind == 0:
        mu = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.45))
        v = np.exp(-((t - mu) / sig) ** 2)
    elif kind == 1:
        mu1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        mu2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        if abs(mu1 - mu2) < 0.1:
            mu2 = (mu2 + 0.5) % 1.0
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        v = w * np.exp(-((t - mu1) / 0.25) ** 2) + (1.0 - w) * np.exp(-((t - mu2) / 0.30) ** 2)
    elif kind == 2:
        a = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        c = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        d = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
        v = (1.0 + a * t + b * t * t + c * t ** 3) * np.exp(d * t)
    elif kind == 3:
        nb = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
        br = np.sort(rng.integers(1, m, size=nb - 1))
        v = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        for lev in rng.uniform(0.15, 3.0, size=nb):
            if lev > 1e-12:
                v[:] *= 0.0
            seg = slice(0, br[0])
            v[seg] += lev
            for j, bnd in enumerate(br):
                seg = slice(bnd, br[j + 1] if j + 1 < len(br) else m)
                v[seg] += rng.uniform(0.1, 3.0)
    else:
        v = rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0, size=m)
        v = np.convolve(v, np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0, mode="same")

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.maximum(v, 1e-6)
    ys = np.log(v)
    ys -= float(np.median(ys))
    return np.clip(ys, -8.0, 8.0)


def _upsample_profile(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    m = p.size
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    t0 = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    t1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    out = np.interp(t1, t0, p)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out_sum = out.sum()
    if out_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(out_sum):
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(out.max())
    if mx > 0.0 and np.isfinite(mx):
        out = out / mx
    return out


def _full_score_fft(p, fft_len):
    n = p.size
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    fx = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_len)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fx * fx, fft_len)[: 2 * n - 1]
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def _full_local_search(x, full_deadline, rng):
    n = x.size
    fft_len = 1
    while fft_len < 2 * n - 1:
        fft_len *= 2
    p = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    ps = p.sum()
    p = p / ps
    best = p.copy()
    best_logp = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
    best_s = _full_score_fft(best, fft_len)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_logp = np.log(best)
        best_s = _full_score_fft(best, fft_len)

    sigma = 0.25
    no_imp = 0
    widths = (4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256)
    tgrid = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    while time.monotonic() < full_deadline:
        op = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
        lp = best_logp.copy()

        if op == 0:
            w = int(widths[int(rng.integers(0, len(widths)))])
            if w > n:
                w = max(1, n // 2)
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            inc = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w)
            if w > 2:
                inc *= np.hanning(w)
            lp[st:st + w] += inc
        elif op == 1:
            nc = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            for _ in range(nc):
                f = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 6.0))
                phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, sigma * 0.35))
                lp += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * tgrid + phase)
        else:
            w1 = int(rng.integers(64, max(65, n // 2)))
            w2 = int(rng.integers(64, max(65, n // 2)))
            s1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w1 + 1)))
            s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w2 + 1)))
            a1 = np.hanning(w1)
            a2 = np.hanning(w2)
            c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0)) * sigma
            c2 = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0)) * sigma
            lp[s1:s1 + w1] += c1 * a1
            lp[s2:s2 + w2] -= c2 * a2

        lp = np.nan_to_num(lp, nan=0.0, posinf=20.0, neginf=-20.0)
        lp -= float(np.median(lp))
        cand = np.exp(lp)
        cs = cand.sum()
        if cs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cs):
            continue
        cand = cand / cs
        s = _full_score_fft(cand, fft_len)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best = cand
            best_logp = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            best_s = s
            no_imp = 0
            sigma = max(0.03, sigma * 0.995)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 120:
                no_imp = 0
                sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.65)
            elif no_imp >= 600:
                no_imp = 0
                sigma = min(1.0, sigma * 1.7)

    mx = float(best.max())
    if mx > 0.0:
        best = best / mx
    return best, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    nfinal = 3809
    parent = None
    try:
        parr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parr.ndim == 1 and parr.size > 0:
            parent = np.clip(parr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(parent)) and 1 <= parent.size <= 10000:
                nfinal = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Valid fallback without relying on optional parent values.
    fallback = np.ones(max(32, nfinal), dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        fs = evaluate_sequence(fallback.tolist())
        if not np.isfinite(fs):
            fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
            fs = evaluate_sequence(fallback.tolist())
    except Exception:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        fs = np.inf
    best_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_exact = float(fs) if np.isfinite(fs) else np.inf

    if parent is not None and parent.size == nfinal:
        try:
            ps = evaluate_sequence(parent.tolist())
            if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_exact:
                best_seq = parent.copy()
                best_exact = float(ps)
        except Exception:
            pass

    coarse_deadline = min(
        deadline - max(120.0, 0.45 * float(budget_s)),
        t0 + min(650.0, 0.55 * float(budget_s)),
    )

    m = 64
    # If final length is very small, coarse length should not exceed it.
    if nfinal < 4 * m:
        m = max(16, min(m, nfinal // 2))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)[:m]
    starts = []

    starts.append(np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64))
    for a in (-4, -3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4):
        starts.append(a * (t - 0.5))
    for a in (-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3):
        for b in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):
            if a == 0 and b == 0:
                continue
            starts.append(a * (t - 0.5) + b * (t - 0.5) ** 2)

    for frac in (0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.4, 0.6):
        y = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        cut = max(1, int(round(frac * m)))
        y[:min(cut, m - 1)] = 1.0
        starts.append(y)
        y2 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        y2[max(1, m - cut):] = 1.0
        starts.append(y2)

    for _ in range(42):
        starts.append(_make_random_profile(rng, m))

    # Use the parent as one additional initial construction, without copying its
    # values into the returned source or literal constants.
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        try:
            pp = np.clip(parent, 0.0, None)
            ps = pp.sum()
            if ps > 0:
                tt = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
                tp = (np.arange(parent.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(parent.size)
                samp = np.interp(tt, tp, pp)
                samp = np.maximum(samp, 1e-12)
                ys = np.log(samp)
                ys -= float(np.median(ys))
                starts.append(np.clip(ys, -8.0, 8.0))
        except Exception:
            pass

    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(y_or_p):
        p = _softmax(np.asarray(y_or_p, dtype=np.float64))
        s = _coarse_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            candidates.append((s, p.copy()))

    for i, y0 in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() > coarse_deadline:
            break
        add_candidate(y0)
        y_opt = _opt_coarse_start(y0, m, beta=35, deadline=coarse_deadline)
        add_candidate(y_opt)

    if not candidates:
        candidates.append((2.0, np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)))

    candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    # Polish the best coarse profiles with direct exact coarse scoring.
    polished = []
    for s, p in candidates[:18]:
        if time.monotonic() > coarse_deadline:
            break
        p2, s2 = _coarse_polish(p, coarse_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(s2):
            polished.append((s2, p2.copy()))
    polished.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    if polished:
        coarse_s, coarse_p = polished[0]
    else:
        coarse_s, coarse_p = candidates[0]

    # Upsample to the working length and validate against the exact evaluator.
    full_p = _upsample_profile(coarse_p, nfinal)
    if full_p.size != nfinal:
        full_p = _upsample_profile(np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64), nfinal)

    full_deadline = deadline - 6.0
    try:
        refined, _ = _full_local_search(full_p, full_deadline, rng)
        full_p = refined
    except Exception:
        pass

    mx = float(full_p.max())
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        full_p = np.ones(nfinal, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        full_p = full_p / mx

    try:
        cand_list = [float(v) for v in full_p]
        cs = evaluate_sequence(cand_list)
        if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_exact:
            best_seq = np.asarray(cand_list, dtype=np.float64)
            best_exact = float(cs)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Final safety path: if the parent was valid, keep the better of parent and
    # constructed result. Always return a non-empty finite nonnegative list.
    try:
        fl = float(full_p.max())
        if fl > 0.0 and np.isfinite(fl):
            out = [float(v) / fl * min(1.0, 1000.0) for v in full_p]
        else:
            out = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        out = [1.0]

    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    out = [0.0 if (not np.isfinite(float(x))) else float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, float(x)))) for x in out]
    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0]

    return out
```
